The name of our state is Assam. Guwahati is the biggest city of Assam.
There is a big river in Assam. The name of the river is the Brahmaputra. The
natural scenery of Assam is very beautiful. The  'Kamakhya' temple is situ-
ated on the hilltop of Nilachal.
